[&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">The <code>langchain.mcp</code> namespace is in beta. Importing from it raises a <code>LangChainBetaWarning</code>.</span></div></div><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1c99crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#features-2" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features"></a><span id="_R_1c99crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>One adapter, any transport</strong>: <code>MCPAdapter</code> infers the transport from its target — a URL, a local script over stdio, an in-process server, an <code>MCPConfig</code> dict for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/connections#multiple-servers" class="link">multiple servers</a>, or a pre-built <code>fastmcp.Client</code>. <code>await adapter.list_tools()</code> returns LangChain tools ready for <code>create_agent</code>. See the <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp" class="link">MCP overview</a>.</li>
@@ -459,25 +459,25 @@
 <li><strong>Authentication through FastMCP</strong>: bearer tokens, full OAuth 2.1 with dynamic client registration, or any <code>httpx.Auth</code>, including <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth#per-user-authentication" class="link">per-user authentication</a> in a deployment. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth" class="link">Authentication</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Richer tool metadata</strong>: each tool carries its MCP provenance under an <code>mcp</code> namespace on its metadata, including annotations such as <code>destructive_hint</code> for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#human-in-the-loop" class="link">gating destructive tools behind approval</a>. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#tool-metadata" class="link">Tool metadata</a>.</li>
 </ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="migrating" aria-labelledby="_R_1s99crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#migrating" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Migrating"></a><span id="_R_1s99crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Migrating</span></h3><span data-as="p">Moving from <code>langchain-mcp-adapters</code>? <code>MultiServerMCPClient</code> is replaced by <code>MCPAdapter</code>, and several features have changed or been removed. See the <a href="/oss/python/migrate/langchain-mcp-adapters" class="link">migration guide</a>.</span></div></div></div>
-<div class="update flex flex-col relative items-start w-full lg:flex-row gap-2 lg:gap-6 py-8 update-container" id="jul-24-2026"><div class="lg:sticky top-(--scroll-mt) group flex flex-col w-full lg:w-[160px] items-start shrink-0 justify-start"><a href="#jul-24-2026" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to changelog: Jul 24, 2026"></a><button type="button" class="cursor-pointer px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm flex items-center grow-0 justify-center font-medium bg-primary/10 text-primary dark:text-primary-light" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="update-label">Jul 24, 2026</button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span><div class="px-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-secondary dark:text-secondary-light mt-3 text-sm" data-component-part="update-tag-list"><span class="inline-block rounded-lg text-sm font-medium" data-component-part="update-tag">deepagents</span></div></div><div class="flex-1 overflow-hidden px-0.5 max-w-full" data-toc-measure="true"><div class="prose-sm" data-component-part="update-content"><h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="deepagents-v0-7-0" aria-labelledby="_R_cb9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#deepagents-v0-7-0" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: deepagents v0.7.0"></a><span id="_R_cb9crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.7.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our <a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">revamped evaluation suite</a> with no quality regression.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="optimizations" aria-labelledby="_R_sb9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#optimizations" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Optimizations"></a><span id="_R_sb9crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Optimizations</span></h3><ul>
-<li><strong>Lean prompts by default</strong>: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop <strong>43%</strong> (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4859</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4979</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#5009</a>)</li>
+<div class="update flex flex-col relative items-start w-full lg:flex-row gap-2 lg:gap-6 py-8 update-container" id="jul-24-2026"><div class="lg:sticky top-(--scroll-mt) group flex flex-col w-full lg:w-[160px] items-start shrink-0 justify-start"><a href="#jul-24-2026" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to changelog: Jul 24, 2026"></a><button type="button" class="cursor-pointer px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm flex items-center grow-0 justify-center font-medium bg-primary/10 text-primary dark:text-primary-light" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="update-label">Jul 24, 2026</button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span><div class="px-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-secondary dark:text-secondary-light mt-3 text-sm" data-component-part="update-tag-list"><span class="inline-block rounded-lg text-sm font-medium" data-component-part="update-tag">deepagents</span></div></div><div class="flex-1 overflow-hidden px-0.5 max-w-full" data-toc-measure="true"><div class="prose-sm" data-component-part="update-content"><h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="deepagents-v0-7-0" aria-labelledby="_R_cb9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#deepagents-v0-7-0" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: deepagents v0.7.0"></a><span id="_R_cb9crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.7.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our <a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">revamped evaluation suite</a> with no quality regression.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="optimizations" aria-labelledby="_R_sb9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#optimizations" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Optimizations"></a><span id="_R_sb9crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Optimizations</span></h3><ul>
+<li><strong>Lean prompts by default</strong>: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop <strong>43%</strong> (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4859</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4979</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#5009</a>)</li>
 </ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-3" aria-labelledby="_R_1cb9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#features-3" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features"></a><span id="_R_1cb9crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
-<li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware" class="link">Override a default middleware instance</a></strong>: A <code>middleware=</code> (or subagent <code>middleware</code>) instance whose <code>.name</code> matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own <code>SummarizationMiddleware(...)</code> to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4251</a>)</li>
-<li><strong>Filesystem tools</strong>: New <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/tools#built-in-harness-tools" class="link"><code>delete</code></a> tool removes a file or recursively removes a directory (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3659" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#3659</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3851" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#3851</a>); <code>write_file</code> now overwrites an existing file instead of erroring (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4109" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4109</a>); <code>FilesystemMiddleware</code> accepts a <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/overview#virtual-filesystem-access" class="link">tool allowlist</a> to expose only selected built-in tools (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4325" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4325</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4698" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4698</a>); and reads and searches are tuned for open models — paginated <code>read_file</code> reports total and remaining lines plus the next <code>offset</code> (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4540" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4540</a>), <code>grep</code>/<code>glob</code> return partial results with a <code>truncated</code> flag instead of hanging on large trees (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4063" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4063</a>), and <code>grep</code> gains a 1,000-match cap with streamed output and optional context lines (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4570" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4570</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4706" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4706</a>).</li>
-<li><strong>More prompt-caching support</strong>: Bedrock prompt caching via the <code>deepagents[aws]</code> extra (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/issues/4108" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4108</a>), and automatic Fireworks prompt-cache session affinity (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4598" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4598</a>).</li>
-<li><strong>NVIDIA support</strong>: A built-in Nemotron 3 Ultra harness profile plus NIM app-origin attribution. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4192" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4192</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4455" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4455</a>)</li>
+<li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware" class="link">Override a default middleware instance</a></strong>: A <code>middleware=</code> (or subagent <code>middleware</code>) instance whose <code>.name</code> matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own <code>SummarizationMiddleware(...)</code> to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4251</a>)</li>
+<li><strong>Filesystem tools</strong>: New <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/tools#built-in-harness-tools" class="link"><code>delete</code></a> tool removes a file or recursively removes a directory (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3659" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#3659</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3851" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#3851</a>); <code>write_file</code> now overwrites an existing file instead of erroring (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4109" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4109</a>); <code>FilesystemMiddleware</code> accepts a <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/overview#virtual-filesystem-access" class="link">tool allowlist</a> to expose only selected built-in tools (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4325" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4325</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4698" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4698</a>); and reads and searches are tuned for open models — paginated <code>read_file</code> reports total and remaining lines plus the next <code>offset</code> (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4540" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4540</a>), <code>grep</code>/<code>glob</code> return partial results with a <code>truncated</code> flag instead of hanging on large trees (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4063" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4063</a>), and <code>grep</code> gains a 1,000-match cap with streamed output and optional context lines (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4570" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4570</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4706" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4706</a>).</li>
+<li><strong>More prompt-caching support</strong>: Bedrock prompt caching via the <code>deepagents[aws]</code> extra (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/issues/4108" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4108</a>), and automatic Fireworks prompt-cache session affinity (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4598" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4598</a>).</li>
+<li><strong>NVIDIA support</strong>: A built-in Nemotron 3 Ultra harness profile plus NIM app-origin attribution. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4192" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4192</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4455" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4455</a>)</li>
 </ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1sb9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#breaking-changes-2" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes"></a><span id="_R_1sb9crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><ul>
-<li><strong>Planning todos are opt-in</strong>: <code>create_deep_agent</code> no longer includes <code>TodoListMiddleware</code> by default, so the <code>write_todos</code> tool, <code>todos</code> state channel, and todo-planning prompt are absent unless restored with <code>middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()]</code>. (The OpenAI Codex harness profile still opts in automatically.) (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4929" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4929</a>)</li>
-<li><strong>Backend compatibility shims removed</strong>: Pass concrete <code>BackendProtocol</code> instances instead of factories, configure <code>StoreBackend</code> with an explicit <code>namespace</code>, and use the current <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> / <code>grep</code> / <code>ReadResult</code> APIs. Removed symbols include <code>BackendFactory</code>, <code>BACKEND_TYPES</code>, <code>FileFormat</code>, and <code>Unset</code>. New files store string <code>FileData.content</code>; older <code>list[str]</code> content stays readable and converts on next write. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4541</a>)</li>
-<li><strong>Output format changes</strong>: Empty <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> output is now <code>No files found</code> instead of <code>[]</code>, and <code>read_file</code> no longer renders a fixed-width <code>cat -n</code>-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4561</a>)</li>
+<li><strong>Planning todos are opt-in</strong>: <code>create_deep_agent</code> no longer includes <code>TodoListMiddleware</code> by default, so the <code>write_todos</code> tool, <code>todos</code> state channel, and todo-planning prompt are absent unless restored with <code>middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()]</code>. (The OpenAI Codex harness profile still opts in automatically.) (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4929" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4929</a>)</li>
+<li><strong>Backend compatibility shims removed</strong>: Pass concrete <code>BackendProtocol</code> instances instead of factories, configure <code>StoreBackend</code> with an explicit <code>namespace</code>, and use the current <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> / <code>grep</code> / <code>ReadResult</code> APIs. Removed symbols include <code>BackendFactory</code>, <code>BACKEND_TYPES</code>, <code>FileFormat</code>, and <code>Unset</code>. New files store string <code>FileData.content</code>; older <code>list[str]</code> content stays readable and converts on next write. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4541</a>)</li>
+<li><strong>Output format changes</strong>: Empty <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> output is now <code>No files found</code> instead of <code>[]</code>, and <code>read_file</code> no longer renders a fixed-width <code>cat -n</code>-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">#4561</a>)</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:</span><div class="prompt my-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 border-stone-100 bg-stone-50 dark:border-stone-800 dark:bg-stone-900 text-sm/[20px] text-stone-900 tracking-[-0.1px] dark:text-stone-50 print:print-color-exact"><div class="not-prose flex min-w-0 items-start gap-2" data-component-part="prompt-content-wrapper"><span aria-hidden="true" class="flex h-[1lh] shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-center" data-component-part="prompt-icon"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! bg-gray-800 dark:bg-gray-100 m-0! size-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/tabler/v3.44.0/outline/arrow-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/tabler/v3.44.0/outline/arrow-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;mask-size:100%"></svg></span><div class="min-w-0" data-component-part="prompt-description"><div class="space-y-4 whitespace-normal"><p>Migrate a deepagents codebase from v0.6.x to v0.7.</p></div></div></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden" data-component-part="prompt-actions-wrapper"><button class="px-2 py-1 flex items-center gap-1 justify-center rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-70 outline-offset-4 text-xs/[16px] whitespace-nowrap font-medium border-transparent bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary-dark" data-component-part="prompt-action-copy-button" type="button" aria-label="Copy prompt"><span aria-hidden="true" class="grid [&amp;&gt;*]:col-start-1 [&amp;&gt;*]:row-start-1 [&amp;&gt;*]:flex [&amp;&gt;*]:items-center [&amp;&gt;*]:justify-center [&amp;&gt;*]:gap-1"><span class="invisible"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-2.5 shrink-0 text-white dark:text-white"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Check"></use></svg>Copied</span><span class="">Copy prompt</span></span><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span></button></div></div></div></div></div>
 <div class="update flex flex-col relative items-start w-full lg:flex-row gap-2 lg:gap-6 py-8 update-container" id="may-12-2026"><div class="lg:sticky top-(--scroll-mt) group flex flex-col w-full lg:w-[160px] items-start shrink-0 justify-start"><a href="#may-12-2026" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to changelog: May 12, 2026"></a><button type="button" class="cursor-pointer px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm flex items-center grow-0 justify-center font-medium bg-primary/10 text-primary dark:text-primary-light" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="update-label">May 12, 2026</button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span><div class="px-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-secondary dark:text-secondary-light mt-3 text-sm" data-component-part="update-tag-list"><span class="inline-block rounded-lg text-sm font-medium" data-component-part="update-tag">deepagents</span></div></div><div class="flex-1 overflow-hidden px-0.5 max-w-full" data-toc-measure="true"><div class="prose-sm" data-component-part="update-content"><h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="deepagents-v0-6-0" aria-labelledby="_R_cd9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#deepagents-v0-6-0" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: deepagents v0.6.0"></a><span id="_R_cd9crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.6.0</span></h2><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters" class="link"><code>CodeInterpreterMiddleware</code></a></strong>: (experimental) <code>deepagents</code> now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.</li>
 <li>Supports <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> in <code>stream_events</code> / <code>astream_events</code>. Refer to the <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a> guide for details.</li>
-<li><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): Deep Agents now uses <code>DeltaChannel</code> for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.</li>
-</ul><div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutWarning"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>Rolling back from v0.6.0 is not supported once threads have persisted.</strong> Deep Agents v0.6.0 changes persisted message history and agent files to <code>DeltaChannel</code>, which writes checkpoints in a new format that earlier versions cannot read. Downgrading to an earlier Deep Agents version switches these channels back to non-delta channels, leaving existing delta checkpoints unreadable and causing incomplete or incorrect state reconstruction. If you need to roll back, use the <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/tree/main/examples/delta-channel-dump" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">delta-channel-dump recovery script</a> to migrate affected threads, or discard them, before downgrading. More generally, avoid switching a persisted channel between delta and non-delta representations. See <a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#version-compatibility-and-rollbacks" class="link">Version compatibility and channel changes</a>.</span></div></div><ul>
+<li><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">blog</a>): Deep Agents now uses <code>DeltaChannel</code> for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.</li>
+</ul><div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutWarning"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>Rolling back from v0.6.0 is not supported once threads have persisted.</strong> Deep Agents v0.6.0 changes persisted message history and agent files to <code>DeltaChannel</code>, which writes checkpoints in a new format that earlier versions cannot read. Downgrading to an earlier Deep Agents version switches these channels back to non-delta channels, leaving existing delta checkpoints unreadable and causing incomplete or incorrect state reconstruction. If you need to roll back, use the <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/tree/main/examples/delta-channel-dump" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">delta-channel-dump recovery script</a> to migrate affected threads, or discard them, before downgrading. More generally, avoid switching a persisted channel between delta and non-delta representations. See <a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#version-compatibility-and-rollbacks" class="link">Version compatibility and channel changes</a>.</span></div></div><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/profiles" class="link">Harness profiles</a></strong>: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (<code>HarnessProfile</code>) that <code>create_deep_agent</code> applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.</li>
-<li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend" class="link"><code>ContextHubBackend</code></a></strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.</li>
+<li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend" class="link"><code>ContextHubBackend</code></a></strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">blog</a>): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.</li>
 </ul></div></div></div>
 <div class="update flex flex-col relative items-start w-full lg:flex-row gap-2 lg:gap-6 py-8 update-container" id="may-12-2026-2"><div class="lg:sticky top-(--scroll-mt) group flex flex-col w-full lg:w-[160px] items-start shrink-0 justify-start"><a href="#may-12-2026-2" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to changelog: May 12, 2026"></a><button type="button" class="cursor-pointer px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm flex items-center grow-0 justify-center font-medium bg-primary/10 text-primary dark:text-primary-light" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="update-label">May 12, 2026</button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span><div class="px-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-secondary dark:text-secondary-light mt-3 text-sm" data-component-part="update-tag-list"><span class="inline-block rounded-lg text-sm font-medium" data-component-part="update-tag">langchain</span></div></div><div class="flex-1 overflow-hidden px-0.5 max-w-full" data-toc-measure="true"><div class="prose-sm" data-component-part="update-content"><h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="langchain-v1-3-0" aria-labelledby="_R_cf9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#langchain-v1-3-0" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: langchain v1.3.0"></a><span id="_R_cf9crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>langchain</code> v1.3.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">This release adds support for <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> in <code>stream_events</code> / <code>astream_events</code> for <code>langchain</code> agents. Refer to the <a href="/oss/python/langchain/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a> guide for details.</span></div></div></div>
 <div class="update flex flex-col relative items-start w-full lg:flex-row gap-2 lg:gap-6 py-8 update-container" id="may-12-2026-3"><div class="lg:sticky top-(--scroll-mt) group flex flex-col w-full lg:w-[160px] items-start shrink-0 justify-start"><a href="#may-12-2026-3" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to changelog: May 12, 2026"></a><button type="button" class="cursor-pointer px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm flex items-center grow-0 justify-center font-medium bg-primary/10 text-primary dark:text-primary-light" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="update-label">May 12, 2026</button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span><div class="px-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-secondary dark:text-secondary-light mt-3 text-sm" data-component-part="update-tag-list"><span class="inline-block rounded-lg text-sm font-medium" data-component-part="update-tag">langgraph</span></div></div><div class="flex-1 overflow-hidden px-0.5 max-w-full" data-toc-measure="true"><div class="prose-sm" data-component-part="update-content"><h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="langgraph-v1-2-0" aria-labelledby="_R_ch9crmhkd74t9_"><a href="#langgraph-v1-2-0" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: langgraph v1.2.0"></a><span id="_R_ch9crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>langgraph</code> v1.2.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">This release adds finer-grained control over node execution (timeouts, error recovery, and graceful shutdown), a new channel type that cuts checkpoint overhead for long-running threads, and a new content-block-centric streaming API (v3) with typed, per-channel projections.</span><ul>
@@ -485,13 +485,13 @@
 <span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong>: A new channel type that stores only the incremental delta at each step rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use <code>snapshot_frequency=K</code> to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.</span>
 </li>
 <li>
-<span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#timeouts" class="link">Per-node timeouts</a></strong>: Pass <code>timeout=</code> to <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>add_node</code></a> to cap how long a single attempt may run. Set a hard wall-clock limit (<code>run_timeout</code>), an idle limit that resets on progress (<code>idle_timeout</code>), or both via <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/TimeoutPolicy" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>TimeoutPolicy</code></a>. When the limit fires, LangGraph raises <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeTimeoutError" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>NodeTimeoutError</code></a>, clears writes from that attempt, and hands off to the retry policy. Async nodes only.</span>
-</li>
-<li>
-<span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#error-handling" class="link">Node-level error handlers</a></strong>: Pass <code>error_handler=</code> to <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>add_node</code></a> to run a recovery function after all retries are exhausted. The handler receives a typed <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeError" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>NodeError</code></a> and can return a <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/Command" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>Command</code></a> to update state and route to a different node, useful for Saga/compensation patterns.</span>
-</li>
-<li>
-<span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#graceful-shutdown" class="link">Graceful shutdown</a></strong>: Stop an in-flight run cooperatively after the current superstep completes, and save a resumable checkpoint. Create a <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/runtime/RunControl" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>RunControl</code></a> and call <code>request_drain()</code> from any thread; the run raises <code>GraphDrained</code> and can be resumed later with the same config.</span>
+<span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#timeouts" class="link">Per-node timeouts</a></strong>: Pass <code>timeout=</code> to <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><code>add_node</code></a> to cap how long a single attempt may run. Set a hard wall-clock limit (<code>run_timeout</code>), an idle limit that resets on progress (<code>idle_timeout</code>), or both via <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/TimeoutPolicy" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><code>TimeoutPolicy</code></a>. When the limit fires, LangGraph raises <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeTimeoutError" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><code>NodeTimeoutError</code></a>, clears writes from that attempt, and hands off to the retry policy. Async nodes only.</span>
+</li>
+<li>
+<span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#error-handling" class="link">Node-level error handlers</a></strong>: Pass <code>error_handler=</code> to <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><code>add_node</code></a> to run a recovery function after all retries are exhausted. The handler receives a typed <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeError" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><code>NodeError</code></a> and can return a <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/Command" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><code>Command</code></a> to update state and route to a different node, useful for Saga/compensation patterns.</span>
+</li>
+<li>
+<span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#graceful-shutdown" class="link">Graceful shutdown</a></strong>: Stop an in-flight run cooperatively after the current superstep completes, and save a resumable checkpoint. Create a <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/runtime/RunControl" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><code>RunControl</code></a> and call <code>request_drain()</code> from any thread; the run raises <code>GraphDrained</code> and can be resumed later with the same config.</span>
 </li>
 <li>
 <span data-as="p"><strong>New event streaming API (beta)</strong>: Pass <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> to <code>stream_events()</code> / <code>astream_events()</code> for a content-block-centric protocol with typed, per-channel projections (<code>run.values</code>, <code>run.messages</code>, <code>run.lifecycle</code>, <code>run.subgraphs</code>) plus opt-in transformers for updates, custom events, checkpoints, tasks, and debug. <code>run.messages</code> yields one <code>ChatModelStream</code> per LLM call with typed sub-projections for text, reasoning, tool calls, and usage. <code>version=&quot;v1&quot;</code> and <code>version=&quot;v2&quot;</code> are unchanged.</span>
```
